# Surah 2 Full-Context Ayah Walk

## 2:0 — بِسْمِ ٱللَّهِ ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ ٱلرَّحِيمِ
### Activated readings
1. **ر ح م B003 (womb), alongside B001 (mercy).** The doubled divine epithets and the surah's later birth, nursing, and kinship laws make mercy the containing medium from which ordered life emerges; I infer nurture precedes command, so the opening names a generative enclosure, not only clemency.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 228 and 233 unexpectedly reactivate the womb-and-nurture image inside the opening mercy.

## 2:2 — ذَٰلِكَ ٱلْكِتَٰبُ لَا رَيْبَ ۛ فِيهِ ۛ هُدًۭى لِّلْمُتَّقِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **ك ت ب B003 (binding inscription) with و ق ي B001 (protective barrier).** Negated disturbance, guidance, and protection form an operational chain; I infer inscription stabilizes conduct, so the book reads as a protective covenantal instrument, not merely a text.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 178, 180, 183, and 282 later make the book's binding and documentary force concrete.

## 2:3 — ٱلَّذِينَ يُؤْمِنُونَ بِٱلْغَيْبِ وَيُقِيمُونَ ٱلصَّلَوٰةَ وَمِمَّا رَزَقْنَٰهُمْ يُنفِقُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ق و م B004 (care and preservation) and ن ف ق B002 (wealth going out).** Hidden trust becomes maintained worship and outgoing provision; I infer unseen commitment is verified by sustained public circulation, so faith reads as an infrastructure of upkeep and release.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 261-274 turn this early circulation into an extended ecology of giving.

## 2:4 — وَٱلَّذِينَ يُؤْمِنُونَ بِمَآ أُنزِلَ إِلَيْكَ وَمَآ أُنزِلَ مِن قَبْلِكَ وَبِٱلْءَاخِرَةِ هُمْ يُوقِنُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ن ز ل B001 (alighting) and ق ب ل B002 (priorness).** Repeated descent links present and prior reception while ء خ ر B001 extends the sequence beyond; I infer revelation occupies successive stations, so certainty concerns one continuous temporal corridor rather than isolated messages.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 136 and 285 later sharpen the corridor by refusing division among messengers.

## 2:5 — أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ عَلَىٰ هُدًۭى مِّن رَّبِّهِمْ ۖ وَأُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ هُمُ ٱلْمُفْلِحُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ف ل ح B003 (tiller splitting ground), beside B005 (lasting success).** Being “upon” guidance follows maintained worship and expenditure; I infer guidance works like cultivation, so flourishing reads as durable yield produced by disciplined cleavage and sowing.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 261 and 265 later supply the seed and garden mechanism latent in flourishing.

## 2:6 — إِنَّ ٱلَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا۟ سَوَآءٌ عَلَيْهِمْ ءَأَنذَرْتَهُمْ أَمْ لَمْ تُنذِرْهُمْ لَا يُؤْمِنُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ك ف ر B001 (covering) and س و ي B001 (equivalence).** Warning and non-warning become equal only after covering blocks reception; I infer the equality is produced by a sealed input channel, so the ayah diagnoses resistant concealment rather than informational shortage.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 7 immediately supplies the sealing mechanism; ayah 74 later materializes it as hardness.

## 2:7 — خَتَمَ ٱللَّهُ عَلَىٰ قُلُوبِهِمْ وَعَلَىٰ سَمْعِهِمْ ۖ وَعَلَىٰٓ أَبْصَٰرِهِمْ غِشَٰوَةٌۭ ۖ وَلَهُمْ عَذَابٌ عَظِيمٌۭ
### Activated readings
1. **خ ت م B003 (securing seal), غ ش و B001 (covering veil), and ق ل ب B004 (turning).** Seal, hearing, sight, and veil form layered closure; I infer repeated refusal has become stabilized orientation, so punishment begins as a perceptual system locked against reversal.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 74-75 later show that hearing can occur while the inward turn remains blocked.

## 2:8 — وَمِنَ ٱلنَّاسِ مَن يَقُولُ ءَامَنَّا بِٱللَّهِ وَبِٱلْيَوْمِ ٱلْءَاخِرِ وَمَا هُم بِمُؤْمِنِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **ق و ل B001 (uttered speech) versus ء م ن B001 (settled trust).** The grammar places voiced claim against denied state; I infer speech can mimic trust without settling it, so belief reads as inward security tested beyond verbal affiliation.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 204-206 later expose polished speech whose hostile inward structure appears in action.

## 2:9 — يُخَٰدِعُونَ ٱللَّهَ وَٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ وَمَا يَخْدَعُونَ إِلَّآ أَنفُسَهُمْ وَمَا يَشْعُرُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **خ د ع B001 (showing other than hidden) and ن ف س B013 (hidden intent).** The attempted outward deception returns upon the inner self; I infer a false interface reshapes its operator, so deceit is self-enclosure before it is successful manipulation of others.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 76-77 later repeat the public/private split and deny that secrecy contains it.

## 2:10 — فِى قُلُوبِهِم مَّرَضٌۭ فَزَادَهُمُ ٱللَّهُ مَرَضًۭا ۖ وَلَهُمْ عَذَابٌ أَلِيمٌۢ بِمَا كَانُوا۟ يَكْذِبُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **م ر ض B005 (dimming and lost clarity) with ز ي د B001 (growth).** Falsehood repeatedly feeds inward dimming; I supply the causal arrow from practiced lying to reduced discernment, so increase reads as endogenous amplification, not an unrelated added illness.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 204 later shows how attractive speech can coexist with the fully developed inward disease.

## 2:11 — وَإِذَا قِيلَ لَهُمْ لَا تُفْسِدُوا۟ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ قَالُوٓا۟ إِنَّمَا نَحْنُ مُصْلِحُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ف س د B001 (departure from balance) against ص ل ح B002 (social settlement).** The mirrored claims contest the same social mechanism; I infer repair language can conceal balance-destroying conduct, so “reform” must be tested by effects on shared ground.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 205 later names crop and progeny as the measurable ground on which this claim fails.

## 2:12 — أَلَآ إِنَّهُمْ هُمُ ٱلْمُفْسِدُونَ وَلَٰكِن لَّا يَشْعُرُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ش ع ر B005 (subtle awareness) with ف س د B001.** The emphatic identification is paired with absent fine perception; I infer corruption can erase its own diagnostic sensitivity, so unawareness is part of the mechanism, not an excuse external to it.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 205 later provides ecological damage as evidence visible even when agents lack self-awareness.

## 2:13 — وَإِذَا قِيلَ لَهُمْ ءَامِنُوا۟ كَمَآ ءَامَنَ ٱلنَّاسُ قَالُوٓا۟ أَنُؤْمِنُ كَمَآ ءَامَنَ ٱلسُّفَهَآءُ ۗ أَلَآ إِنَّهُمْ هُمُ ٱلسُّفَهَآءُ وَلَٰكِن لَّا يَعْلَمُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **س ف ه B001 (light unstable motion) versus ء م ن B001 (settled security).** Their insult reverses upon them through the contrast between instability and settled trust; I infer contempt protects a wavering identity, so folly reads as structural volatility rather than low intelligence.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 142 later links the same instability to agitation over a changed orientation.

## 2:14 — وَإِذَا لَقُوا۟ ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ قَالُوٓا۟ ءَامَنَّا وَإِذَا خَلَوْا۟ إِلَىٰ شَيَٰطِينِهِمْ قَالُوٓا۟ إِنَّا مَعَكُمْ إِنَّمَا نَحْنُ مُسْتَهْزِءُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ل ق ي B004 (face-to-face meeting), خ ل و B002 (private aloneness), and ه ز ء B001.** Opposed social settings trigger opposed speech; I infer belonging is being managed by audience, so mockery functions as private loyalty certification.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 76 later reproduces the same encounter/private-withdrawal architecture almost exactly.

## 2:15 — ٱللَّهُ يَسْتَهْزِئُ بِهِمْ وَيَمُدُّهُمْ فِى طُغْيَٰنِهِمْ يَعْمَهُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **م د د B004 (extended respite), ط غ ي B002 (overflow), and ع م ه B001 (bewildered wavering).** Extension operates inside overflow rather than outside it; I infer uncorrected motion becomes its own exposure, so the response reads as letting pretended control disclose actual disorientation.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 204-206 later show the mature endpoint: impressive speech, destructive action, and prideful refusal.

## 2:16 — أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ ٱلَّذِينَ ٱشْتَرَوُا۟ ٱلضَّلَٰلَةَ بِٱلْهُدَىٰ فَمَا رَبِحَت تِّجَٰرَتُهُمْ وَمَا كَانُوا۟ مُهْتَدِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **ش ر ي B001 (exchange), ر ب ح B001 (trade profit), and ض ل ل B003 (losing).** The commercial sequence prices guidance away and yields no gain; I infer choices accumulate as a portfolio, so misguidance reads as a knowingly structured but self-defeating exchange.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 86, 175, and 207 later reuse exchange language for radically different purchases of the self.

## 2:17 — مَثَلُهُمْ كَمَثَلِ ٱلَّذِى ٱسْتَوْقَدَ نَارًۭا فَلَمَّآ أَضَآءَتْ مَا حَوْلَهُۥ ذَهَبَ ٱللَّهُ بِنُورِهِمْ وَتَرَكَهُمْ فِى ظُلُمَٰتٍۢ لَّا يُبْصِرُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **و ق د B001 (kindling), ض و ء B003 (looking by firelight from darkness), and ن و ر B005 (beacon).** Brief local illumination lacks durable orientation; I infer borrowed flare was mistaken for a guiding source, so loss of light reads as exposure of dependence rather than simple darkness.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 255-257 later distinguish sustaining divine light from transitions into and out of darkness.

## 2:18 — صُمٌّۢ بُكْمٌ عُمْىٌۭ فَهُمْ لَا يَرْجِعُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ر ج ع B001 (return) anchors the sensory triad.** Deafness, muteness, and blindness remove reception, articulation, and navigation; I infer return requires all three feedback channels, so the line diagnoses a closed corrective loop.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 171 later repeats the triad and adds absent understanding, confirming the loop's cognitive function.

## 2:19 — أَوْ كَصَيِّبٍۢ مِّنَ ٱلسَّمَآءِ فِيهِ ظُلُمَٰتٌۭ وَرَعْدٌۭ وَبَرْقٌۭ يَجْعَلُونَ أَصَٰبِعَهُمْ فِىٓ ءَاذَانِهِم مِّنَ ٱلصَّوَٰعِقِ حَذَرَ ٱلْمَوْتِ ۚ وَٱللَّهُ مُحِيطٌۢ بِٱلْكَٰفِرِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **ص و ب B001 (descending rain), ح ذ ر B001 (guarded fear), and ح و ط B001 (enclosure).** They block the warning-bearing sound while already enclosed by what they fear; I infer selective sensory control cannot alter total exposure, so caution becomes futile self-insulation.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 243 later reverses the logic: flight undertaken from fear of death leads directly through death to life.

## 2:20 — يَكَادُ ٱلْبَرْقُ يَخْطَفُ أَبْصَٰرَهُمْ ۖ كُلَّمَآ أَضَآءَ لَهُم مَّشَوْا۟ فِيهِ وَإِذَآ أَظْلَمَ عَلَيْهِمْ قَامُوا۟ ۚ وَلَوْ شَآءَ ٱللَّهُ لَذَهَبَ بِسَمْعِهِمْ وَأَبْصَٰرِهِمْ ۚ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ عَلَىٰ كُلِّ شَىْءٍۢ قَدِيرٌۭ
### Activated readings
1. **م ش ي B001 (walking), ق و م B016 (stoppage), and خ ط ف B001 (snatching).** Motion depends on intermittent brightness and freezes at darkness; I infer opportunistic response lacks an internal bearing, so their path is stimulus-driven locomotion, not guidance.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 142-150 later contrast this flickering movement with a commanded, stable direction.

## 2:21 — يَٰٓأَيُّهَا ٱلنَّاسُ ٱعْبُدُوا۟ رَبَّكُمُ ٱلَّذِى خَلَقَكُمْ وَٱلَّذِينَ مِن قَبْلِكُمْ لَعَلَّكُمْ تَتَّقُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ر ب ب B002 (nurture and completion), ع ب د B001 (servitude), and و ق ي B002 (protective caution).** Creation of present and prior people grounds worship in sustained formation; I infer recognizing nurture generates protective alignment, so service is response to ongoing care, not naked subjection.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 233 and 286 later connect nurture, burden limits, and protection in legal form.

## 2:22 — ٱلَّذِى جَعَلَ لَكُمُ ٱلْأَرْضَ فِرَٰشًۭا وَٱلسَّمَآءَ بِنَآءًۭ وَأَنزَلَ مِنَ ٱلسَّمَآءِ مَآءًۭ فَأَخْرَجَ بِهِۦ مِنَ ٱلثَّمَرَٰتِ رِزْقًۭا لَّكُمْ ۖ فَلَا تَجْعَلُوا۟ لِلَّهِ أَندَادًۭا وَأَنتُمْ تَعْلَمُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ف ر ش B001 (spreading as bedding), ب ن ي B001 (constructed building), and ر ز ق B003 (rain-provision).** Ground, canopy, descent, emergence, and fruit form a vertical provisioning system; I infer dependency reveals unity of source, so rivals are denied by ecology, not only assertion.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 164 later expands the same integrated earth-sky-water-life mechanism.

## 2:23 — وَإِن كُنتُمْ فِى رَيْبٍۢ مِّمَّا نَزَّلْنَا عَلَىٰ عَبْدِنَا فَأْتُوا۟ بِسُورَةٍۢ مِّن مِّثْلِهِۦ وَٱدْعُوا۟ شُهَدَآءَكُم مِّن دُونِ ٱللَّهِ إِن كُنتُمْ صَٰدِقِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **ر ي ب B001 (disturbing doubt), س و ر B001 (enclosing elevated wall), and ش ه د B001 (present witness).** Doubt is moved into a bounded production-and-witness test; I infer accountable comparison disciplines suspicion, so uncertainty must become public demonstration rather than remain atmospheric.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 282 later develops witnessing and writing into a full anti-doubt procedure.

## 2:24 — فَإِن لَّمْ تَفْعَلُوا۟ وَلَن تَفْعَلُوا۟ فَٱتَّقُوا۟ ٱلنَّارَ ٱلَّتِى وَقُودُهَا ٱلنَّاسُ وَٱلْحِجَارَةُ ۖ أُعِدَّتْ لِلْكَٰفِرِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **و ق ي B001 (protective barrier), و ق د B002 (fuel), and ح ج ر B001 (stone).** Failed action shifts from challenge to guarding against becoming material of combustion; I infer persistent covering converts persons and hardness into fuel, so warning asks for changed material disposition.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 74 later compares hearts to stones yet notes that some stones split, flow, or descend.

## 2:25 — وَبَشِّرِ ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ وَعَمِلُوا۟ ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ أَنَّ لَهُمْ جَنَّٰتٍۢ تَجْرِى مِن تَحْتِهَا ٱلْأَنْهَٰرُ ۖ كُلَّمَا رُزِقُوا۟ مِنْهَا مِن ثَمَرَةٍۢ رِّزْقًۭا ۙ قَالُوا۟ هَٰذَا ٱلَّذِى رُزِقْنَا مِن قَبْلُ ۖ وَأُتُوا۟ بِهِۦ مُتَشَٰبِهًۭا ۖ وَلَهُمْ فِيهَآ أَزْوَٰجٌۭ مُّطَهَّرَةٌۭ ۖ وَهُمْ فِيهَا خَٰلِدُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ج ن ن B001 (concealed enclosure), ج ر ي B001 (flow), and ش ب ه B001 (resemblance).** Hidden gardens continuously yield recognizable-yet-renewed fruit; I infer continuity need not mean repetition, so lasting life reads as stable belonging with inexhaustible variation.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 264-266 later use rain-fed gardens to expose how moral quality changes yield.

## 2:26 — ۞ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ لَا يَسْتَحْىِۦٓ أَن يَضْرِبَ مَثَلًۭا مَّا بَعُوضَةًۭ فَمَا فَوْقَهَا ۚ فَأَمَّا ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ فَيَعْلَمُونَ أَنَّهُ ٱلْحَقُّ مِن رَّبِّهِمْ ۖ وَأَمَّا ٱلَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا۟ فَيَقُولُونَ مَاذَآ أَرَادَ ٱللَّهُ بِهَٰذَا مَثَلًۭا ۘ يُضِلُّ بِهِۦ كَثِيرًۭا وَيَهْدِى بِهِۦ كَثِيرًۭا ۚ وَمَا يُضِلُّ بِهِۦٓ إِلَّا ٱلْفَٰسِقِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **ض ر ب B001 (striking/setting), م ث ل B011 (lesson-sign), and ف س ق B001 (breaking out).** One small comparison produces opposite trajectories; I infer examples reveal the reader's prior relation to bounds, so guidance or loss is a diagnostic interaction, not arbitrary content.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 17-20, 171, and 261-266 later make parables recurrent tests of perceptual and moral posture.

## 2:27 — ٱلَّذِينَ يَنقُضُونَ عَهْدَ ٱللَّهِ مِنۢ بَعْدِ مِيثَٰقِهِۦ وَيَقْطَعُونَ مَآ أَمَرَ ٱللَّهُ بِهِۦٓ أَن يُوصَلَ وَيُفْسِدُونَ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ ۚ أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ هُمُ ٱلْخَٰسِرُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ن ق ض B001 (unravelling), و ث ق B001 (binding), ق ط ع B001 (cutting), and و ص ل B001 (joining).** The sequence reverses binding and connection into rupture and earthly imbalance; I infer covenant is connective infrastructure, so loss is systemic disassembly.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 83-85 and 204-205 later show covenant rupture as violence against social and ecological continuity.

## 2:28 — كَيْفَ تَكْفُرُونَ بِٱللَّهِ وَكُنتُمْ أَمْوَٰتًۭا فَأَحْيَٰكُمْ ۖ ثُمَّ يُمِيتُكُمْ ثُمَّ يُحْيِيكُمْ ثُمَّ إِلَيْهِ تُرْجَعُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ك ف ر B001 (covering), ح ي ي B001 (living), and ر ج ع B001 (return).** The repeated death-life sequence encloses present denial within received and future animation; I infer life itself is disclosed evidence, so covering God means suppressing the trajectory embodied by one's existence.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 243 and 258-260 later stage death-life reversals as visible demonstrations.

## 2:29 — هُوَ ٱلَّذِى خَلَقَ لَكُم مَّا فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ جَمِيعًۭا ثُمَّ ٱسْتَوَىٰٓ إِلَى ٱلسَّمَآءِ فَسَوَّىٰهُنَّ سَبْعَ سَمَٰوَٰتٍۢ ۚ وَهُوَ بِكُلِّ شَىْءٍ عَلِيمٌۭ
### Activated readings
1. **ج م ع B001 (gathering), س و ي B002 (sound completion), and ع ل م B001 (clear knowledge).** Earthly totality precedes ordered heavenly plurality; I infer knowledge coordinates levels without fragmentation, so creation reads as gathered provision within a completed architecture.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 164 later populates this architecture with circulating winds, clouds, water, vessels, and life.

## 2:30 — وَإِذْ قَالَ رَبُّكَ لِلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةِ إِنِّى جَاعِلٌۭ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ خَلِيفَةًۭ ۖ قَالُوٓا۟ أَتَجْعَلُ فِيهَا مَن يُفْسِدُ فِيهَا وَيَسْفِكُ ٱلدِّمَآءَ وَنَحْنُ نُسَبِّحُ بِحَمْدِكَ وَنُقَدِّسُ لَكَ ۖ قَالَ إِنِّىٓ أَعْلَمُ مَا لَا تَعْلَمُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **خ ل ف B001 (coming after/succession), ف س د B001, and س ف ك B001 (spilling).** Appointment is voiced against predicted earthly violence; I infer succession entails risky delegated agency whose capacity exceeds the forecast, so the human role is a contested trust, not an automatic honor.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 251 and 282 later show delegated order through restraint, knowledge, testimony, and documentation.

## 2:31 — وَعَلَّمَ ءَادَمَ ٱلْأَسْمَآءَ كُلَّهَا ثُمَّ عَرَضَهُمْ عَلَى ٱلْمَلَٰئِكَةِ فَقَالَ أَنۢبِـُٔونِى بِأَسْمَآءِ هَٰٓؤُلَآءِ إِن كُنتُمْ صَٰدِقِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **س م و B005 (raised designation), ع ر ض B001 (presentation), and ن ب ء B001 (consequential report).** Names are taught, displayed, then requested under a truth condition; I infer naming enables accountable differentiation, so knowledge is demonstrated relational competence, not stored labels.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 282 later turns naming, writing, and witnessing into accountable social memory.

## 2:32 — قَالُوا۟ سُبْحَٰنَكَ لَا عِلْمَ لَنَآ إِلَّا مَا عَلَّمْتَنَآ ۖ إِنَّكَ أَنتَ ٱلْعَلِيمُ ٱلْحَكِيمُ
### Activated readings
1. **ع ل م B001 (clarified knowing) and ح ك م B001 (restraining judgment).** Admitted limit sits beside attributed wisdom; I infer sound judgment includes bounding one's epistemic reach, so “we do not know” is itself a disciplined knowledge-act.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 216 and 232 later repeat the asymmetry between divine knowledge and human non-knowledge in decisions.

## 2:33 — قَالَ يَٰٓـَٔادَمُ أَنۢبِئْهُم بِأَسْمَآئِهِمْ ۖ فَلَمَّآ أَنۢبَأَهُم بِأَسْمَآئِهِمْ قَالَ أَلَمْ أَقُل لَّكُمْ إِنِّىٓ أَعْلَمُ غَيْبَ ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَٱلْأَرْضِ وَأَعْلَمُ مَا تُبْدُونَ وَمَا كُنتُمْ تَكْتُمُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ن ب ء B001 (weighty report), غ ي ب B001 (hidden), ب د و B001 (manifest), and ك ت م B001 (concealment).** Adam's report is framed by complete access to outer and inner states; I infer truthful naming bridges hidden structure and visible expression, so the test concerns disclosure.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 77 and 284 later universalize the manifest/concealed accountability introduced here.

## 2:34 — وَإِذْ قُلْنَا لِلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةِ ٱسْجُدُوا۟ لِءَادَمَ فَسَجَدُوٓا۟ إِلَّآ إِبْلِيسَ أَبَىٰ وَٱسْتَكْبَرَ وَكَانَ مِنَ ٱلْكَٰفِرِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **س ج د B001 (lowering), ء ب ي B001 (refusal), ك ب ر B001 (self-enlargement), and ك ف ر B001.** Bodily lowering is opposed by inward inflation and covering; I infer refusal protects a comparative self-image, so disbelief appears as enacted posture before doctrinal statement.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 206 later repeats the mechanism: admonition triggers self-magnifying attachment to wrongdoing.

## 2:35 — وَقُلْنَا يَٰٓـَٔادَمُ ٱسْكُنْ أَنتَ وَزَوْجُكَ ٱلْجَنَّةَ وَكُلَا مِنْهَا رَغَدًا حَيْثُ شِئْتُمَا وَلَا تَقْرَبَا هَٰذِهِ ٱلشَّجَرَةَ فَتَكُونَا مِنَ ٱلظَّٰلِمِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **س ك ن B001 (settled dwelling), ر غ د B001 (ample ease), and ق ر ب B001 (approach).** Broad permission contains one proximity-boundary; I infer freedom remains habitable through a small restraint, so wrongdoing is read as destabilizing dwelling by crossing the condition that preserves it.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 187 later uses the same “do not approach” logic for boundaries that preserve a lawful field.

## 2:36 — فَأَزَلَّهُمَا ٱلشَّيْطَٰنُ عَنْهَا فَأَخْرَجَهُمَا مِمَّا كَانَا فِيهِ ۖ وَقُلْنَا ٱهْبِطُوا۟ بَعْضُكُمْ لِبَعْضٍ عَدُوٌّۭ ۖ وَلَكُمْ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ مُسْتَقَرٌّۭ وَمَتَٰعٌ إِلَىٰ حِينٍۢ
### Activated readings
1. **ز ل ل B001 (slip), خ ر ج B001 (exit), ه ب ط B001 (descent), and ق ر ر B001 (settlement).** A local slip becomes expulsion, descent, hostility, then temporary earthly stability; I infer transgression reconfigures space and relation, so the fall is a new social ecology, not only relocation.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 204-205 later define earthly corruption through damaged cultivation and progeny.

## 2:37 — فَتَلَقَّىٰٓ ءَادَمُ مِن رَّبِّهِۦ كَلِمَٰتٍۢ فَتَابَ عَلَيْهِ ۚ إِنَّهُۥ هُوَ ٱلتَّوَّابُ ٱلرَّحِيمُ
### Activated readings
1. **ل ق ي B011 (receiving speech) and ت و ب B001 (return).** Received words precede reciprocal return; I infer language supplies a route back after spatial and moral dislocation, so repentance is reorientation enabled by an addressed relation.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 128 and 285-286 later place return inside taught communal prayer and response.

## 2:38 — قُلْنَا ٱهْبِطُوا۟ مِنْهَا جَمِيعًۭا ۖ فَإِمَّا يَأْتِيَنَّكُم مِّنِّى هُدًۭى فَمَن تَبِعَ هُدَاىَ فَلَا خَوْفٌ عَلَيْهِمْ وَلَا هُمْ يَحْزَنُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ه د ي B002 (intended direction), ت ب ع B003 (stepwise tracking), خ و ف B001, and ح ز ن B001.** Descent is followed by directional guidance whose tracking removes future fear and backward grief; I infer guidance repairs temporal as well as spatial disorientation.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 142-150 later embody this repair through repeated turning toward a stable direction.

## 2:39 — وَٱلَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا۟ وَكَذَّبُوا۟ بِـَٔايَٰتِنَآ أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ أَصْحَٰبُ ٱلنَّارِ ۖ هُمْ فِيهَا خَٰلِدُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ك ف ر B001 (covering), ك ذ ب B002 (declaring false), and ص ح ب B001 (close association).** Covering signs matures into active falsification and then durable companionship with fire; I infer repeated stance forms habitat, so outcome mirrors cultivated association.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 81-82 later contrast two enduring companionships formed by encompassing error or sound action.

## 2:40 — يَٰبَنِىٓ إِسْرَٰٓءِيلَ ٱذْكُرُوا۟ نِعْمَتِىَ ٱلَّتِىٓ أَنْعَمْتُ عَلَيْكُمْ وَأَوْفُوا۟ بِعَهْدِىٓ أُوفِ بِعَهْدِكُمْ وَإِيَّٰىَ فَٱرْهَبُونِ
### Activated readings
1. **ذ ك ر B003 (bringing to mind), و ف ي B001 (full completion), and ع ه د B001 (binding commitment).** Remembered favor precedes reciprocal fulfillment; I infer memory supplies covenantal motivation, so fear is bounded by a prior economy of gift and completion.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 83-85 later show what covenantal amnesia does to mutual life and dwelling.

## 2:41 — وَءَامِنُوا۟ بِمَآ أَنزَلْتُ مُصَدِّقًۭا لِّمَا مَعَكُمْ وَلَا تَكُونُوٓا۟ أَوَّلَ كَافِرٍۭ بِهِۦ ۖ وَلَا تَشْتَرُوا۟ بِـَٔايَٰتِى ثَمَنًۭا قَلِيلًۭا وَإِيَّٰىَ فَٱتَّقُونِ
### Activated readings
1. **ص د ق B001 (truth that holds), ش ر ي B001 (exchange), and ق ل ل B001 (smallness).** Confirmation is opposed to selling signs cheaply; I infer prior possession creates special recognition-cost, so first rejection reads as devaluing what one's own resources should verify.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 89-90 later expose recognition followed by jealous exchange of the self.

## 2:42 — وَلَا تَلْبِسُوا۟ ٱلْحَقَّ بِٱلْبَٰطِلِ وَتَكْتُمُوا۟ ٱلْحَقَّ وَأَنتُمْ تَعْلَمُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ل ب س B003 (confusing mixture), ب ط ل B001 (voidness), and ك ت م B001.** Mixing and concealment are coordinated while knowledge remains; I infer distortion works by diluting visibility rather than erasing all truth, so knowingly blurred presentation is itself suppression.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 282-283 later counter this mixture with exact writing, witnessing, and non-concealment.

## 2:43 — وَأَقِيمُوا۟ ٱلصَّلَوٰةَ وَءَاتُوا۟ ٱلزَّكَوٰةَ وَٱرْكَعُوا۟ مَعَ ٱلرَّٰكِعِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **ق و م B004 (preserving), ز ك و B001 (growth), and ر ك ع B001 (bending).** Sustained worship, released growth, and collective lowering form one public discipline; I infer vertical humility maintains horizontal circulation, so ritual and redistribution are mutually stabilizing.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 177 later embeds the same prayer-giving pair within a wider social definition of righteousness.

## 2:44 — ۞ أَتَأْمُرُونَ ٱلنَّاسَ بِٱلْبِرِّ وَتَنسَوْنَ أَنفُسَكُمْ وَأَنتُمْ تَتْلُونَ ٱلْكِتَٰبَ ۚ أَفَلَا تَعْقِلُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ن س ي B002 (abandoning), ت ل و B001 (following in sequence), and ع ق ل B001 (restraining understanding).** Recitation proceeds while the self is left behind; I infer true following must pull the speaker into the commanded sequence, so reason is the tether closing the gap between instruction and embodiment.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 121 later defines proper recitation as giving the book its due following.

## 2:45 — وَٱسْتَعِينُوا۟ بِٱلصَّبْرِ وَٱلصَّلَوٰةِ ۚ وَإِنَّهَا لَكَبِيرَةٌ إِلَّا عَلَى ٱلْخَٰشِعِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **ص ب ر B001 (self-restraint), ص ل و B003 (prescribed worship), and خ ش ع B001 (low submission).** Restraint and prayer become assistance, yet weight varies by posture; I infer humility changes load-bearing capacity, so difficulty is relational rather than fixed in the practice alone.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 153-157 later show this load-bearing system operating under loss and affliction.

## 2:46 — ٱلَّذِينَ يَظُنُّونَ أَنَّهُم مُّلَٰقُوا۟ رَبِّهِمْ وَأَنَّهُمْ إِلَيْهِ رَٰجِعُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ظ ن ن B001 (certainty from indication), ل ق ي B004 (face-to-face meeting), and ر ج ع B001.** Anticipated encounter and return explain humbled endurance; I infer future meeting reorganizes present weight, so ظن here reads as operative conviction rather than weak conjecture.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 249 later explicitly makes the same expected meeting generate courage under numerical weakness.

## 2:47 — يَٰبَنِىٓ إِسْرَٰٓءِيلَ ٱذْكُرُوا۟ نِعْمَتِىَ ٱلَّتِىٓ أَنْعَمْتُ عَلَيْكُمْ وَأَنِّى فَضَّلْتُكُمْ عَلَى ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **ذ ك ر B003, ن ع م B001 (bestowed favor), and ف ض ل B003 (bounty).** Repeated remembrance frames distinction as received surplus; I infer privilege creates obligation rather than essence, so elevation reads as accountable benefaction.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 122-124 later repeat the claim and immediately test whether covenant reaches wrongdoing descendants.

## 2:48 — وَٱتَّقُوا۟ يَوْمًۭا لَّا تَجْزِى نَفْسٌ عَن نَّفْسٍۢ شَيْـًۭٔا وَلَا يُقْبَلُ مِنْهَا شَفَٰعَةٌۭ وَلَا يُؤْخَذُ مِنْهَا عَدْلٌۭ وَلَا هُمْ يُنصَرُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ج ز ي B002 (standing in for another), ش ف ع B002 (joining as advocate), and ع د ل B003 (ransom-equivalent).** Every substitution channel is negated in sequence; I infer accountability cannot be outsourced, so protection means preparing the self, not assembling transferable social capital.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 123 repeats the structure with reordered advocacy and benefit, reinforcing channel-by-channel closure.

## 2:49 — وَإِذْ نَجَّيْنَٰكُم مِّنْ ءَالِ فِرْعَوْنَ يَسُومُونَكُمْ سُوٓءَ ٱلْعَذَابِ يُذَبِّحُونَ أَبْنَآءَكُمْ وَيَسْتَحْيُونَ نِسَآءَكُمْ ۚ وَفِى ذَٰلِكُم بَلَآءٌۭ مِّن رَّبِّكُمْ عَظِيمٌۭ
### Activated readings
1. **ن ج و B001 (deliverance by separation), س و م B002 (imposed harm), and ب ل و B002 (testing that discloses).** Rescue separates a people from selective generational violence; I infer affliction exposes both oppressor and surviving community, so trial includes the moral demands created by deliverance.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 83-85 later show the delivered community reenacting violence and expulsion internally.

## 2:50 — وَإِذْ فَرَقْنَا بِكُمُ ٱلْبَحْرَ فَأَنجَيْنَٰكُمْ وَأَغْرَقْنَآ ءَالَ فِرْعَوْنَ وَأَنتُمْ تَنظُرُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ف ر ق B004 (cleaving), ن ج و B001, غ ر ق B001 (sinking), and ن ظ ر B001.** One cleft becomes passage for one group and engulfment for another under direct sight; I infer the same opened medium discriminates by relation and movement, so deliverance and judgment are one spatial event.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 249 later repeats water as a discriminating passage that reveals a community's composition.

## 2:51 — وَإِذْ وَٰعَدْنَا مُوسَىٰٓ أَرْبَعِينَ لَيْلَةًۭ ثُمَّ ٱتَّخَذْتُمُ ٱلْعِجْلَ مِنۢ بَعْدِهِۦ وَأَنتُمْ ظَٰلِمُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **و ع د B004 (mutual appointment), ع ج ل B002 (calf) shadowed by B001 (haste), and ء خ ذ B010 (taking for oneself).** Appointed absence is answered by hurried self-made attachment; I infer waiting pressure converts into manufactured immediacy, so the calf also figures impatience with unseen covenant.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 93 later makes the attachment bodily: the calf is “drunk into” their hearts.

## 2:52 — ثُمَّ عَفَوْنَا عَنكُم مِّنۢ بَعْدِ ذَٰلِكَ لَعَلَّكُمْ تَشْكُرُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ع ف و B001 (wiping away guilt), with B007 (growth when left), and ش ك ر B002 (little provision showing effect).** Pardon after rupture creates unconsumed capacity; I infer erased liability should reappear as grateful growth, so forgiveness is released moral surplus, not mere cancellation.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 219 and 237 later use عفو as expendable surplus and generous relinquishment.

## 2:53 — وَإِذْ ءَاتَيْنَا مُوسَى ٱلْكِتَٰبَ وَٱلْفُرْقَانَ لَعَلَّكُمْ تَهْتَدُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ك ت ب B003 (binding inscription), ف ر ق B001 (distinguishing), and ه د ي B002 (direction).** Written binding is paired with a separator; I infer guidance requires distinctions that can govern action, so scripture reads as both durable norm and discriminating instrument.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 185 later joins guidance, clear signs, and الفرقان within the fasting month.

## 2:54 — وَإِذْ قَالَ مُوسَىٰ لِقَوْمِهِۦ يَٰقَوْمِ إِنَّكُمْ ظَلَمْتُمْ أَنفُسَكُم بِٱتِّخَاذِكُمُ ٱلْعِجْلَ فَتُوبُوٓا۟ إِلَىٰ بَارِئِكُمْ فَٱقْتُلُوٓا۟ أَنفُسَكُمْ ذَٰلِكُمْ خَيْرٌۭ لَّكُمْ عِندَ بَارِئِكُمْ فَتَابَ عَلَيْكُمْ ۚ إِنَّهُۥ هُوَ ٱلتَّوَّابُ ٱلرَّحِيمُ
### Activated readings
1. **ب ر ء B001 (bringing into being), B002 (separation), ت و ب B001, and ق ت ل B001.** Return to the Creator is expressed through severe separation from the self implicated in calf-making; I infer re-creation requires cutting the collective attachment, so repentance is communal deconstitution and return.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 178 later places killing within a legal field where pardon, brotherhood, and merciful mitigation remain possible.

## 2:55 — وَإِذْ قُلْتُمْ يَٰمُوسَىٰ لَن نُّؤْمِنَ لَكَ حَتَّىٰ نَرَى ٱللَّهَ جَهْرَةًۭ فَأَخَذَتْكُمُ ٱلصَّٰعِقَةُ وَأَنتُمْ تَنظُرُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ج ه ر B002 (plain manifestation), ء خ ذ B001 (seizing), and ن ظ ر B001.** A demand for unmediated visibility meets an overwhelming visible seizure while they look; I infer coercive evidence destroys the conditions of trusting response, so sight cannot simply substitute for faith.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 118 and 260 later distinguish sign-demand from a request aimed at inward settling.

## 2:56 — ثُمَّ بَعَثْنَٰكُم مِّنۢ بَعْدِ مَوْتِكُمْ لَعَلَّكُمْ تَشْكُرُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ب ع ث B001 (rousing stillness), م و ت B001, and ش ك ر B002.** Reanimation follows the failed demand for sight; I infer restored life is itself the answer that should become visible in conduct, so gratitude is resurrection's intended social evidence.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 243 and 259 later expand rousing after death into communal and material demonstrations.

## 2:57 — وَظَلَّلْنَا عَلَيْكُمُ ٱلْغَمَامَ وَأَنزَلْنَا عَلَيْكُمُ ٱلْمَنَّ وَٱلسَّلْوَىٰ ۖ كُلُوا۟ مِن طَيِّبَٰتِ مَا رَزَقْنَٰكُمْ ۖ وَمَا ظَلَمُونَا وَلَٰكِن كَانُوٓا۟ أَنفُسَهُمْ يَظْلِمُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ظ ل ل B004 (protective shelter), م ن ن B002 (sustaining strength), and ط ي ب B001 (pure good).** Cover, descended food, and permission form a protected supply system; I infer misuse cannot diminish its source but deforms recipients, so self-wrong is failed participation in provision.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 172-173 and 267 later refine provision through purity, gratitude, necessity, and quality of giving.

## 2:58 — وَإِذْ قُلْنَا ٱدْخُلُوا۟ هَٰذِهِ ٱلْقَرْيَةَ فَكُلُوا۟ مِنْهَا حَيْثُ شِئْتُمْ رَغَدًۭا وَٱدْخُلُوا۟ ٱلْبَابَ سُجَّدًۭا وَقُولُوا۟ حِطَّةٌۭ نَّغْفِرْ لَكُمْ خَطَٰيَٰكُمْ ۚ وَسَنَزِيدُ ٱلْمُحْسِنِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **د خ ل B001 (entering), ب و ب B001 (door), س ج د B001, and ح ط ط B002 (remission).** Abundant entry is routed through a specific threshold, lowered posture, and lowering-word; I infer access becomes restorative when bodily and verbal orientation match, so the gate is a moral transition.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 189 later generalizes the image: enter houses through their doors rather than evade proper approach.

## 2:59 — فَبَدَّلَ ٱلَّذِينَ ظَلَمُوا۟ قَوْلًا غَيْرَ ٱلَّذِى قِيلَ لَهُمْ فَأَنزَلْنَا عَلَى ٱلَّذِينَ ظَلَمُوا۟ رِجْزًۭا مِّنَ ٱلسَّمَآءِ بِمَا كَانُوا۟ يَفْسُقُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ب د ل B001 (substitution), ق و ل B001, and ف س ق B001 (breaking out).** Substituted speech reverses the prescribed lowering at the threshold; I infer verbal alteration performs departure from the relation the command establishes, so language-change is covenantal exit, not harmless paraphrase.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 181 and 211 later treat post-reception substitution as transferred liability and exchanged favor.

## 2:60 — ۞ وَإِذِ ٱسْتَسْقَىٰ مُوسَىٰ لِقَوْمِهِۦ فَقُلْنَا ٱضْرِب بِّعَصَاكَ ٱلْحَجَرَ ۖ فَٱنفَجَرَتْ مِنْهُ ٱثْنَتَا عَشْرَةَ عَيْنًۭا ۖ قَدْ عَلِمَ كُلُّ أُنَاسٍۢ مَّشْرَبَهُمْ ۖ كُلُوا۟ وَٱشْرَبُوا۟ مِن رِّزْقِ ٱللَّهِ وَلَا تَعْثَوْا۟ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ مُفْسِدِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **ف ج ر B001 (bursting flow), ع ي ن B001 (spring), and ع ل م B001.** Distributed springs give each group a known intake; I infer ordered access prevents provision from becoming conflict, so water is social allocation as well as miracle.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 164 later places water inside a planet-wide circulation of life.

## 2:61 — وَإِذْ قُلْتُمْ يَٰمُوسَىٰ لَن نَّصْبِرَ عَلَىٰ طَعَامٍۢ وَٰحِدٍۢ فَٱدْعُ لَنَا رَبَّكَ يُخْرِجْ لَنَا مِمَّا تُنۢبِتُ ٱلْأَرْضُ مِنۢ بَقْلِهَا وَقِثَّآئِهَا وَفُومِهَا وَعَدَسِهَا وَبَصَلِهَا ۖ قَالَ أَتَسْتَبْدِلُونَ ٱلَّذِى هُوَ أَدْنَىٰ بِٱلَّذِى هُوَ خَيْرٌ ۚ ٱهْبِطُوا۟ مِصْرًۭا فَإِنَّ لَكُم مَّا سَأَلْتُمْ ۗ وَضُرِبَتْ عَلَيْهِمُ ٱلذِّلَّةُ وَٱلْمَسْكَنَةُ وَبَآءُو بِغَضَبٍۢ مِّنَ ٱللَّهِ ۗ ذَٰلِكَ بِأَنَّهُمْ كَانُوا۟ يَكْفُرُونَ بِـَٔايَٰتِ ٱللَّهِ وَيَقْتُلُونَ ٱلنَّبِيِّۦنَ بِغَيْرِ ٱلْحَقِّ ۗ ذَٰلِكَ بِمَا عَصَوا۟ وَّكَانُوا۟ يَعْتَدُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ب د ل B001 and د ن و B001 (nearness/lowness).** Requested variety becomes downward exchange; I infer appetite can revalue liberated provision through familiarity, so culinary complaint reveals a wider pattern of rejecting signs and bounds.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 267 later makes the quality of what one chooses morally diagnostic.

## 2:62 — إِنَّ ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ وَٱلَّذِينَ هَادُوا۟ وَٱلنَّصَٰرَىٰ وَٱلصَّٰبِـِٔينَ مَنْ ءَامَنَ بِٱللَّهِ وَٱلْيَوْمِ ٱلْءَاخِرِ وَعَمِلَ صَٰلِحًۭا فَلَهُمْ أَجْرُهُمْ عِندَ رَبِّهِمْ وَلَا خَوْفٌ عَلَيْهِمْ وَلَا هُمْ يَحْزَنُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ء م ن B001 and ص ل ح B001.** Named affiliations are followed by an open conditional of trust and sound action; I infer labels do not replace enacted settlement, so reward attaches to a cross-group mechanism.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 111-113 later reject exclusive salvation claims and mutual nullification.

## 2:63 — وَإِذْ أَخَذْنَا مِيثَٰقَكُمْ وَرَفَعْنَا فَوْقَكُمُ ٱلطُّورَ خُذُوا۟ مَآ ءَاتَيْنَٰكُم بِقُوَّةٍۢ وَٱذْكُرُوا۟ مَا فِيهِ لَعَلَّكُمْ تَتَّقُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **و ث ق B001, ر ف ع B001, and ق و ي B001.** Elevated mountain spatializes covenant weight; I infer strong taking means carrying its memory into conduct, so protection grows from sustained load-bearing.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 93 repeats the scene and exposes hearing severed from obedience.

## 2:64 — ثُمَّ تَوَلَّيْتُم مِّنۢ بَعْدِ ذَٰلِكَ ۖ فَلَوْلَا فَضْلُ ٱللَّهِ عَلَيْكُمْ وَرَحْمَتُهُۥ لَكُنتُم مِّنَ ٱلْخَٰسِرِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **و ل ي B001 (turning) and ف ض ل B001 (surplus).** Turning away should yield loss, but surplus mercy interrupts it; I infer grace is unearned remainder that keeps return possible.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 105 later names mercy's particularizing freedom and abundant surplus.

## 2:65 — وَلَقَدْ عَلِمْتُمُ ٱلَّذِينَ ٱعْتَدَوْا۟ مِنكُمْ فِى ٱلسَّبْتِ فَقُلْنَا لَهُمْ كُونُوا۟ قِرَدَةً خَٰسِـِٔينَ
### Activated readings
1. **ع د و B001 (crossing a bound) and خ س ء B001 (driven-away abasement).** Boundary-crossing becomes an exposed degraded state; I infer habitual evasion reshapes social identity, so judgment manifests the posture already enacted.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 229-231 later repeatedly define injustice as transgressing explicit limits.

## 2:66 — فَجَعَلْنَٰهَا نَكَٰلًۭا لِّمَا بَيْنَ يَدَيْهَا وَمَا خَلْفَهَا وَمَوْعِظَةًۭ لِّلْمُتَّقِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **ن ك ل B001 (deterrent restraint), ي د ي B001, and خ ل ف B001.** The event faces both temporal directions; I infer witnessed consequence can restrain future repetition, so history becomes a protective boundary.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 211 later warns that received signs can still be exchanged away.

## 2:67 — وَإِذْ قَالَ مُوسَىٰ لِقَوْمِهِۦٓ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ يَأْمُرُكُمْ أَن تَذْبَحُوا۟ بَقَرَةًۭ ۖ قَالُوٓا۟ أَتَتَّخِذُنَا هُزُوًۭا ۖ قَالَ أَعُوذُ بِٱللَّهِ أَنْ أَكُونَ مِنَ ٱلْجَٰهِلِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **ه ز ء B001 and ج ه ل B001.** A direct command is reframed as ridicule; I infer suspicious reception creates complexity before details do, so ignorance is failure to grant serious address.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 231 later forbids turning divine signs into mockery within family law.

## 2:68 — قَالُوا۟ ٱدْعُ لَنَا رَبَّكَ يُبَيِّن لَّنَا مَا هِىَ ۚ قَالَ إِنَّهُۥ يَقُولُ إِنَّهَا بَقَرَةٌۭ لَّا فَارِضٌۭ وَلَا بِكْرٌ عَوَانٌۢ بَيْنَ ذَٰلِكَ ۖ فَٱفْعَلُوا۟ مَا تُؤْمَرُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ب ي ن B001 (clarifying separation) and ع و ن B002 (middle age).** Clarification places the animal between extremes; I infer adequate specification should terminate delay, so “between” functions as actionable precision.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 143 later develops وسط as a communal position fitted for testimony.

## 2:69 — قَالُوا۟ ٱدْعُ لَنَا رَبَّكَ يُبَيِّن لَّنَا مَا لَوْنُهَا ۚ قَالَ إِنَّهُۥ يَقُولُ إِنَّهَا بَقَرَةٌۭ صَفْرَآءُ فَاقِعٌۭ لَّوْنُهَا تَسُرُّ ٱلنَّٰظِرِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **ل و ن B001 (color), ف ق ع B001 (intense brightness), and س ر ر B001 (inward delight).** Exterior color enters viewers as pleasure; I infer visible distinction is memorable guidance, so specification recruits affect as recognition.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 138 later shifts coloring from animal surface to divine formation of a community.

## 2:70 — قَالُوا۟ ٱدْعُ لَنَا رَبَّكَ يُبَيِّن لَّنَا مَا هِىَ إِنَّ ٱلْبَقَرَ تَشَٰبَهَ عَلَيْنَا وَإِنَّآ إِن شَآءَ ٱللَّهُ لَمُهْتَدُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ش ب ه B001 (resemblance) and ه د ي B001.** Repeated questioning converts a simple category into confusing similarity; I infer delay multiplies indistinguishable options, so guidance now means escape from self-produced ambiguity.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 118 later links similar hearts to repeated demands for additional signs.

## 2:71 — قَالَ إِنَّهُۥ يَقُولُ إِنَّهَا بَقَرَةٌۭ لَّا ذَلُولٌۭ تُثِيرُ ٱلْأَرْضَ وَلَا تَسْقِى ٱلْحَرْثَ مُسَلَّمَةٌۭ لَّا شِيَةَ فِيهَا ۚ قَالُوا۟ ٱلْـَٰٔنَ جِئْتَ بِٱلْحَقِّ ۚ فَذَبَحُوهَا وَمَا كَادُوا۟ يَفْعَلُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **س ل م B001 (wholeness) and و ش ي B001 (marking).** Unworked, sound, unmarked singularity finally compels action; I infer their search for certainty raised compliance cost, so delayed truth-recognition exposes reluctance.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 216 later states directly that required action may remain disliked despite known good.

## 2:72 — وَإِذْ قَتَلْتُمْ نَفْسًۭا فَٱدَّٰرَْٰٔتُمْ فِيهَا ۖ وَٱللَّهُ مُخْرِجٌۭ مَّا كُنتُمْ تَكْتُمُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **د ر ء B001 (mutual pushing away), ك ت م B001, and خ ر ج B001.** Blame is pushed among parties while concealed fact is forced outward; I infer accusation can disperse responsibility but not erase it.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 283 later locates concealed testimony's guilt in the heart.

## 2:73 — فَقُلْنَا ٱضْرِبُوهُ بِبَعْضِهَا ۚ كَذَٰلِكَ يُحْىِ ٱللَّهُ ٱلْمَوْتَىٰ وَيُرِيكُمْ ءَايَٰتِهِۦ لَعَلَّكُمْ تَعْقِلُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ب ع ض B001 (part), ح ي ي B001, and ع ق ل B001.** A part reconnects hidden murder to revivified witness; I infer material contact makes denied relation legible, so reason is awakened through reconstructive sign.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 260 later uses divided birds and reassembly to settle a heart.

## 2:74 — ثُمَّ قَسَتْ قُلُوبُكُم مِّنۢ بَعْدِ ذَٰلِكَ فَهِىَ كَٱلْحِجَارَةِ أَوْ أَشَدُّ قَسْوَةًۭ ۚ وَإِنَّ مِنَ ٱلْحِجَارَةِ لَمَا يَتَفَجَّرُ مِنْهُ ٱلْأَنْهَٰرُ ۚ وَإِنَّ مِنْهَا لَمَا يَشَّقَّقُ فَيَخْرُجُ مِنْهُ ٱلْمَآءُ ۚ وَإِنَّ مِنْهَا لَمَا يَهْبِطُ مِنْ خَشْيَةِ ٱللَّهِ ۗ وَمَا ٱللَّهُ بِغَٰفِلٍ عَمَّا تَعْمَلُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ق س و B001 (hardness), ف ج ر B001, ش ق ق B001, and ه ب ط B001.** Stone is corrected as a comparison because it can flow, split, or descend; I infer true hardness is blocked responsiveness, not material density.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 264-265 later contrast bare stone with receptive soil under the same rain.

## 2:75 — ۞ أَفَتَطْمَعُونَ أَن يُؤْمِنُوا۟ لَكُمْ وَقَدْ كَانَ فَرِيقٌۭ مِّنْهُمْ يَسْمَعُونَ كَلَٰمَ ٱللَّهِ ثُمَّ يُحَرِّفُونَهُۥ مِنۢ بَعْدِ مَا عَقَلُوهُ وَهُمْ يَعْلَمُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **س م ع B003 (understanding/compliance), ح ر ف B001 (edge-turning), and ع ق ل B001.** Distortion occurs after comprehension; I infer turning a word to its edge is strategic, so failed belief cannot be blamed on unclear reception.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 282 later protects obligations through exact recording and multiple witnesses.

## 2:76 — وَإِذَا لَقُوا۟ ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ قَالُوٓا۟ ءَامَنَّا وَإِذَا خَلَا بَعْضُهُمْ إِلَىٰ بَعْضٍۢ قَالُوٓا۟ أَتُحَدِّثُونَهُم بِمَا فَتَحَ ٱللَّهُ عَلَيْكُمْ لِيُحَآجُّوكُم بِهِۦ عِندَ رَبِّكُمْ ۚ أَفَلَا تَعْقِلُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ف ت ح B001 (opening), ح ج ج B001 (argument), and خ ل و B002.** Received opening is resealed in private for fear of accountability; I infer knowledge is treated as adversarial property, so secrecy reverses revelation's function.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 159-160 later make disclosure itself part of repaired repentance.

## 2:77 — أَوَلَا يَعْلَمُونَ أَنَّ ٱللَّهَ يَعْلَمُ مَا يُسِرُّونَ وَمَا يُعْلِنُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **س ر ر B001 (hidden interior) and ع ل ن B001 (manifest publicity).** Opposed disclosure states fall within one knowledge; I infer privacy changes human access, not divine accountability.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 271 and 274 later permit both hidden and public giving while judging their quality.

## 2:78 — وَمِنْهُمْ أُمِّيُّونَ لَا يَعْلَمُونَ ٱلْكِتَٰبَ إِلَّآ أَمَانِىَّ وَإِنْ هُمْ إِلَّا يَظُنُّونَ
### Activated readings
1. **م ن ي B001 (wish) and ظ ن ن B003 (uncertain supposition).** Wishes occupy the place of textual knowledge; I infer desire supplies counterfeit content, so illiteracy here is dependency on imagined guarantees.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 94-96 later test the death-wish and expose attachment to prolonged life.

## 2:79 — فَوَيْلٌۭ لِّلَّذِينَ يَكْتُبُونَ ٱلْكِتَٰبَ بِأَيْدِيهِمْ ثُمَّ يَقُولُونَ هَٰذَا مِنْ عِندِ ٱللَّهِ لِيَشْتَرُوا۟ بِهِۦ ثَمَنًۭا قَلِيلًۭا ۖ فَوَيْلٌۭ لَّهُم مِّمَّا كَتَبَتْ أَيْدِيهِمْ وَوَيْلٌۭ لَّهُم مِّمَّا يَكْسِبُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ك ت ب B002 (written text), ي د ي B001 (hands), and ك س ب B001 (acquisition).** Handwriting, divine attribution, sale, and earnings expose a fabrication chain; I infer material authorship transfers liability to its makers.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 282 later rehabilitates writing as accountable service rather than profitable falsification.

## 2:80 — وَقَالُوا۟ لَن تَمَسَّنَا ٱلنَّارُ إِلَّآ أَيَّامًۭا مَّعْدُودَةًۭ ۚ قُلْ أَتَّخَذْتُمْ عِندَ ٱللَّهِ عَهْدًۭا فَلَن يُخْلِفَ ٱللَّهُ عَهْدَهُۥٓ ۖ أَمْ تَقُولُونَ عَلَى ٱللَّهِ مَا لَا تَعْلَمُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **م س س B001 (touch), ع د د B001 (count), and ع ه د B001.** A quantified punishment claim is tested for covenantal source; I infer precision without warrant is fabricated assurance.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 203 and 282 later contrast legitimate counted times and named terms with this unsupported count.

## 2:81 — بَلَىٰ مَن كَسَبَ سَيِّئَةًۭ وَأَحَٰطَتْ بِهِۦ خَطِيٓـَٔتُهُۥ فَأُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ أَصْحَٰبُ ٱلنَّارِ ۖ هُمْ فِيهَا خَٰلِدُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ك س ب B001 and ح و ط B001 (enclosure).** Acquired wrong grows from possession into surrounding environment; I infer repeated earning builds habitat, so fire-companionship begins as moral enclosure.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 284 later returns every hidden or visible acquisition to accounting.

## 2:82 — وَٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ وَعَمِلُوا۟ ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ أَصْحَٰبُ ٱلْجَنَّةِ ۖ هُمْ فِيهَا خَٰلِدُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ص ل ح B001, ص ح ب B001, and ج ن ن B001.** Sound actions produce enduring companionship with a protected enclosure; I infer practice forms belonging, so garden is the habitat corresponding to repaired relation.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 265 later pictures giving from settled selves as a reliably yielding garden.

## 2:83 — وَإِذْ أَخَذْنَا مِيثَٰقَ بَنِىٓ إِسْرَٰءِيلَ لَا تَعْبُدُونَ إِلَّا ٱللَّهَ وَبِٱلْوَٰلِدَيْنِ إِحْسَانًۭا وَذِى ٱلْقُرْبَىٰ وَٱلْيَتَٰمَىٰ وَٱلْمَسَٰكِينِ وَقُولُوا۟ لِلنَّاسِ حُسْنًۭا وَأَقِيمُوا۟ ٱلصَّلَوٰةَ وَءَاتُوا۟ ٱلزَّكَوٰةَ ثُمَّ تَوَلَّيْتُمْ إِلَّا قَلِيلًۭا مِّنكُمْ وَأَنتُم مُّعْرِضُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **و ث ق B001 and ح س ن B001.** Exclusive worship expands through kin, vulnerable people, speech, prayer, and giving; I infer covenant is a social care network, so turning away dismantles several linked obligations at once.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 177 later restates righteousness as the same integrated belief-and-care structure.

## 2:84 — وَإِذْ أَخَذْنَا مِيثَٰقَكُمْ لَا تَسْفِكُونَ دِمَآءَكُمْ وَلَا تُخْرِجُونَ أَنفُسَكُم مِّن دِيَٰرِكُمْ ثُمَّ أَقْرَرْتُمْ وَأَنتُمْ تَشْهَدُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **س ف ك B001, خ ر ج B001, and ن ف س B012 (the very self).** Bloodshed and expulsion are reflexively “your selves”; I infer communal membership makes violence self-directed, so dwelling and body share covenantal protection.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 217 later ranks expulsion from the sanctuary above isolated combat.

## 2:85 — ثُمَّ أَنتُمْ هَٰٓؤُلَآءِ تَقْتُلُونَ أَنفُسَكُمْ وَتُخْرِجُونَ فَرِيقًۭا مِّنكُم مِّن دِيَٰرِهِمْ تَظَٰهَرُونَ عَلَيْهِم بِٱلْإِثْمِ وَٱلْعُدْوَٰنِ وَإِن يَأْتُوكُمْ أُسَٰرَىٰ تُفَٰدُوهُمْ وَهُوَ مُحَرَّمٌ عَلَيْكُمْ إِخْرَاجُهُمْ ۚ أَفَتُؤْمِنُونَ بِبَعْضِ ٱلْكِتَٰبِ وَتَكْفُرُونَ بِبَعْضٍۢ
### Activated readings
1. **ب ع ض B001, ك ف ر B001, and ف د ي B001.** Rescue of captives coexists with producing captivity; I infer selective observance repairs downstream harm while preserving its cause, so partial belief is a self-contradicting system.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 208 later commands entry into peace “altogether,” directly correcting partitioned obedience.

## 2:86 — أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ ٱلَّذِينَ ٱشْتَرَوُا۟ ٱلْحَيَوٰةَ ٱلدُّنْيَا بِٱلْءَاخِرَةِ ۖ فَلَا يُخَفَّفُ عَنْهُمُ ٱلْعَذَابُ وَلَا هُمْ يُنصَرُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ش ر ي B001 and د ن و B001.** Near life is purchased at the price of the later; I infer partial obedience discounts delayed consequence, so temporal preference is cast as ruinous exchange.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 201 later models a request that refuses the near/later trade-off.

## 2:87 — وَلَقَدْ ءَاتَيْنَا مُوسَى ٱلْكِتَٰبَ وَقَفَّيْنَا مِنۢ بَعْدِهِۦ بِٱلرُّسُلِ ۖ وَءَاتَيْنَا عِيسَى ٱبْنَ مَرْيَمَ ٱلْبَيِّنَٰتِ وَأَيَّدْنَٰهُ بِرُوحِ ٱلْقُدُسِ ۗ أَفَكُلَّمَا جَآءَكُمْ رَسُولٌۢ بِمَا لَا تَهْوَىٰٓ أَنفُسُكُمُ ٱسْتَكْبَرْتُمْ فَفَرِيقًۭا كَذَّبْتُمْ وَفَرِيقًۭا تَقْتُلُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ق ف و B001 (following behind), ه و ي B001 (inclining desire), and ك ب ر B001.** Messenger succession meets self-desire, producing denial or killing; I infer appetite becomes a filter over continuity.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 253 later repeats messenger differentiation and post-sign conflict.

## 2:88 — وَقَالُوا۟ قُلُوبُنَا غُلْفٌۢ ۚ بَل لَّعَنَهُمُ ٱللَّهُ بِكُفْرِهِمْ فَقَلِيلًۭا مَّا يُؤْمِنُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **غ ل ف B001 (covering sheath) and ك ف ر B001.** Claimed protected hearts are recast as self-covering exclusion; I infer insulation from address is not neutral incapacity but cultivated distance.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 260 later presents a heart seeking settled assurance without sealing itself.

## 2:89 — وَلَمَّا جَآءَهُمْ كِتَٰبٌۭ مِّنْ عِندِ ٱللَّهِ مُصَدِّقٌۭ لِّمَا مَعَهُمْ وَكَانُوا۟ مِن قَبْلُ يَسْتَفْتِحُونَ عَلَى ٱلَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا۟ فَلَمَّا جَآءَهُم مَّا عَرَفُوا۟ كَفَرُوا۟ بِهِۦ ۚ فَلَعْنَةُ ٱللَّهِ عَلَى ٱلْكَٰفِرِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **ف ت ح B001, ع ر ف B001 (recognition), and ك ف ر B001.** The hoped-for opening is covered when recognized; I infer anticipation was tied to advantage, so fulfillment becomes intolerable when it redistributes status.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 146 later compares this recognition to recognizing one's own children.

## 2:90 — بِئْسَمَا ٱشْتَرَوْا۟ بِهِۦٓ أَنفُسَهُمْ أَن يَكْفُرُوا۟ بِمَآ أَنزَلَ ٱللَّهُ بَغْيًا أَن يُنَزِّلَ ٱللَّهُ مِن فَضْلِهِۦ عَلَىٰ مَن يَشَآءُ مِنْ عِبَادِهِۦ ۖ فَبَآءُو بِغَضَبٍ عَلَىٰ غَضَبٍۢ
### Activated readings
1. **ش ر ي B001, ب غ ي B001 (seeking beyond right), and ف ض ل B003.** The self is sold because bounty reaches another; I infer envy converts gift-distribution into grievance, so disbelief is a status-protecting purchase.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 247 later rejects wealth-status as the criterion for distributed authority.

## 2:91 — وَإِذَا قِيلَ لَهُمْ ءَامِنُوا۟ بِمَآ أَنزَلَ ٱللَّهُ قَالُوا۟ نُؤْمِنُ بِمَآ أُنزِلَ عَلَيْنَا وَيَكْفُرُونَ بِمَا وَرَآءَهُۥ وَهُوَ ٱلْحَقُّ مُصَدِّقًۭا لِّمَا مَعَهُمْ ۗ قُلْ فَلِمَ تَقْتُلُونَ أَنۢبِيَآءَ ٱللَّهِ مِن قَبْلُ إِن كُنتُم مُّؤْمِنِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **و ر ي B001 (behind/hidden), ص د ق B001, and ق ت ل B001.** “Ours” is exposed by prior violence against its own messengers; I infer possessive belief conceals internal contradiction.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 136 later replaces possessive selection with non-differentiation.

## 2:92 — ۞ وَلَقَدْ جَآءَكُم مُّوسَىٰ بِٱلْبَيِّنَٰتِ ثُمَّ ٱتَّخَذْتُمُ ٱلْعِجْلَ مِنۢ بَعْدِهِۦ وَأَنتُمْ ظَٰلِمُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ب ي ن B001 and ء خ ذ B010.** Clear signs are followed by taking a substitute; I infer temporal proximity intensifies agency, so evidence alone cannot prevent self-made attachment.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 211 later generalizes the danger of exchanging favor after it arrives.

## 2:93 — وَإِذْ أَخَذْنَا مِيثَٰقَكُمْ وَرَفَعْنَا فَوْقَكُمُ ٱلطُّورَ خُذُوا۟ مَآ ءَاتَيْنَٰكُم بِقُوَّةٍۢ وَٱسْمَعُوا۟ ۖ قَالُوا۟ سَمِعْنَا وَعَصَيْنَا وَأُشْرِبُوا۟ فِى قُلُوبِهِمُ ٱلْعِجْلَ بِكُفْرِهِمْ
### Activated readings
1. **س م ع B003, ع ص ي B001, and ش ر ب B001.** Hearing is paired with disobedience while the calf is imbibed inwardly; I infer repeated attachment becomes internal nourishment, blocking compliance.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 285 later answers with “we heard and obeyed,” reversing this fracture.

## 2:94 — قُلْ إِن كَانَتْ لَكُمُ ٱلدَّارُ ٱلْءَاخِرَةُ عِندَ ٱللَّهِ خَالِصَةًۭ مِّن دُونِ ٱلنَّاسِ فَتَمَنَّوُا۟ ٱلْمَوْتَ إِن كُنتُمْ صَٰدِقِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **خ ل ص B001 (exclusive purity), م ن ي B001, and ص د ق B001.** Exclusive claim is tested by desire for its supposed destination; I infer truth should align stated privilege and actual appetite.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 96 immediately exposes the opposite desire: maximal extension of life.

## 2:95 — وَلَن يَتَمَنَّوْهُ أَبَدًۢا بِمَا قَدَّمَتْ أَيْدِيهِمْ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ عَلِيمٌۢ بِٱلظَّٰلِمِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **ق د م B001 (sending ahead) and ي د ي B001.** Prior acts stand ahead of their agents and govern desire; I infer deeds construct the future one fears.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 110 later turns “sending ahead” positively as good found with God.

## 2:96 — وَلَتَجِدَنَّهُمْ أَحْرَصَ ٱلنَّاسِ عَلَىٰ حَيَوٰةٍۢ ... وَٱللَّهُ بَصِيرٌۢ بِمَا يَعْمَلُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ح ر ص B001 (intense grasping), ع م ر B001 (life-extension), and ز ح ز ح B001 (removal).** Desired longevity cannot shift punishment; I infer quantity of time cannot repair its moral direction.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 281 later relocates value from delay to what each self has earned.

## 2:97 — قُلْ مَن كَانَ عَدُوًّۭا لِّجِبْرِيلَ ... وَهُدًۭى وَبُشْرَىٰ لِلْمُؤْمِنِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **ن ز ل B002, ق ل ب B001, and ص د ق B001.** Descent reaches the heart while confirming what lies before it; I infer the messenger is a conduit of continuity, so hostility to him misidentifies the source-relation.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 285 later displays communal reception of the same descent.

## 2:98 — مَن كَانَ عَدُوًّۭا لِّلَّهِ وَمَلَٰٓئِكَتِهِۦ وَرُسُلِهِۦ وَجِبْرِيلَ وَمِيكَىٰلَ فَإِنَّ ٱللَّهَ عَدُوٌّۭ لِّلْكَٰفِرِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **ع د و B001 and ك ف ر B001.** Hostility toward one messenger is placed within the whole network; I infer selective enmity ruptures source, carriers, and messages together.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 285 later binds God, angels, books, and messengers into one confession.

## 2:99 — وَلَقَدْ أَنزَلْنَآ إِلَيْكَ ءَايَٰتٍۭ بَيِّنَٰتٍۢ ۖ وَمَا يَكْفُرُ بِهَآ إِلَّا ٱلْفَٰسِقُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ب ي ن B001, ك ف ر B001, and ف س ق B001.** Clear signs are covered only through prior boundary-exit; I infer rejection discloses posture more than evidential absence.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 209 later names slipping after clear signs as the same post-clarity problem.

## 2:100 — أَوَكُلَّمَا عَٰهَدُوا۟ عَهْدًۭا نَّبَذَهُۥ فَرِيقٌۭ مِّنْهُم ۚ بَلْ أَكْثَرُهُمْ لَا يُؤْمِنُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ع ه د B001 and ن ب ذ B001 (casting away).** Each binding is answered by disposal; I infer repetition converts breach into institutional habit.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 101 next materializes the cast book behind their backs.

## 2:101 — وَلَمَّا جَآءَهُمْ رَسُولٌۭ مِّنْ عِندِ ٱللَّهِ ... كَأَنَّهُمْ لَا يَعْلَمُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ن ب ذ B001, و ر ي B001, and ظ ه ر B001.** The book is thrown behind the bodily surface that should carry it; I infer spatial displacement performs feigned ignorance.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 282 later makes carrying knowledge a public writing duty.

## 2:102 — وَٱتَّبَعُوا۟ مَا تَتْلُوا۟ ٱلشَّيَٰطِينُ ... لَوْ كَانُوا۟ يَعْلَمُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **س ح ر B001 (concealed manipulation), ف ر ق B001, and ض ر ر B001.** Taught technique separates spouses while disclaimers expose its test; I infer knowledge detached from benefit becomes anti-social control, so learning itself is morally directional.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 220-223 later regulate household relations around repair, consent, and protected intimacy.

## 2:103 — وَلَوْ أَنَّهُمْ ءَامَنُوا۟ وَٱتَّقَوْا۟ لَمَثُوبَةٌۭ مِّنْ عِندِ ٱللَّهِ خَيْرٌۭ ۖ لَّوْ كَانُوا۟ يَعْلَمُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **ث و ب B001 (returning reward), و ق ي B001, and خ ي ر B001.** Harmful purchase is contrasted with protective return; I infer restraint redirects the economy of learning toward durable benefit.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 261-274 later elaborate returns that multiply without relational harm.

## 2:104 — يَٰٓأَيُّهَا ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ لَا تَقُولُوا۟ رَٰعِنَا وَقُولُوا۟ ٱنظُرْنَا وَٱسْمَعُوا۟
### Activated readings
1. **ر ع ي B001 (attending/care), ن ظ ر B002 (granting attention), and س م ع B003.** A vulnerable expression is replaced while the communicative function remains; I infer wording must protect shared reception, so linguistic ethics includes foreseeable misuse.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 235 later permits indirect speech only within recognizable honorable wording.

## 2:105 — مَّا يَوَدُّ ٱلَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا۟ ... وَٱللَّهُ ذُو ٱلْفَضْلِ ٱلْعَظِيمِ
### Activated readings
1. **خ ص ص B001 (particularizing) and ف ض ل B003.** Others' resentment meets freely distributed bounty; I infer grace is not governed by rival claims.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 247 later applies selection beyond inherited wealth and status.

## 2:106 — ۞ مَا نَنسَخْ مِنْ ءَايَةٍ أَوْ نُنسِهَا نَأْتِ بِخَيْرٍۢ مِّنْهَآ أَوْ مِثْلِهَآ ...
### Activated readings
1. **ن س خ B001 (transferring/replacing), ن س ي B002, and م ث ل B001.** Removal is paired with equivalent or better arrival; I infer continuity can be preserved through changed form, so replacement is managed succession, not loss of control.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 142-150 later embody continuity through changed prayer-direction.

## 2:107 — أَلَمْ تَعْلَمْ أَنَّ ٱللَّهَ لَهُۥ مُلْكُ ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَٱلْأَرْضِ ...
### Activated readings
1. **م ل ك B001 (possession/authority), و ل ي B001, and ن ص ر B001.** Total dominion grounds replacement and excludes alternate patrons; I infer authority over the whole validates reordering within it.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 255 later expands this dominion into sustaining knowledge and preservation.

## 2:108 — أَمْ تُرِيدُونَ أَن تَسْـَٔلُوا۟ رَسُولَكُمْ ... فَقَدْ ضَلَّ سَوَآءَ ٱلسَّبِيلِ
### Activated readings
1. **س ء ل B001, ب د ل B001, and س و ي B006 (even middle).** Repetitive demands risk exchanging trust for covering; I infer questioning can become a route-switch rather than inquiry.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 189 and 215-222 later model questions answered by actionable ethical redirection.

## 2:109 — وَدَّ كَثِيرٌۭ مِّنْ أَهْلِ ٱلْكِتَٰبِ ... إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ عَلَىٰ كُلِّ شَىْءٍۢ قَدِيرٌۭ
### Activated readings
1. **ح س د B001 (envying removal), ع ف و B001, and ص ف ح B001 (turning the page).** Envious reversal is answered by pardon and page-turning; I infer restraint preserves agency until a new command arrives.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 190-194 later specify when restraint yields to bounded combat.

## 2:110 — وَأَقِيمُوا۟ ٱلصَّلَوٰةَ ... تَجِدُوهُ عِندَ ٱللَّهِ
### Activated readings
1. **ق د م B001, و ج د B001, and ن ف س B011.** Good sent ahead for the self is later found; I infer practice stores value beyond immediate visibility.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 261-274 later detail how giving survives or is erased by subsequent conduct.

## 2:111 — وَقَالُوا۟ لَن يَدْخُلَ ٱلْجَنَّةَ ... قُلْ هَاتُوا۟ بُرْهَٰنَكُمْ
### Activated readings
1. **م ن ي B001 and ب ر ه ن B001 (decisive proof).** Exclusive wishes are forced into evidential form; I infer communal desire cannot authorize destination.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 112 immediately replaces label-based access with surrendered, excellent action.

## 2:112 — بَلَىٰ مَنْ أَسْلَمَ وَجْهَهُۥ لِلَّهِ وَهُوَ مُحْسِنٌۭ ...
### Activated readings
1. **س ل م B001, و ج ه B001 (facing), and ح س ن B001.** Whole surrender is spatialized as directed face and enacted excellence; I infer orientation must become beneficial form.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 142-150 later turn facing into a communal test and practice.

## 2:113 — وَقَالَتِ ٱلْيَهُودُ ... فَٱللَّهُ يَحْكُمُ بَيْنَهُمْ ...
### Activated readings
1. **ل ي س B001, م ث ل B001, and ح ك م B001.** Reciprocal nullification mirrors itself despite shared reading; I infer polemic symmetry reveals failure of scripture to restrain faction.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 213 later identifies revelation's judging role amid precisely such dispute.

## 2:114 — وَمَنْ أَظْلَمُ مِمَّن مَّنَعَ مَسَٰجِدَ ٱللَّهِ ... وَسَعَىٰ فِى خَرَابِهَآ
### Activated readings
1. **م ن ع B001 (blocking), ذ ك ر B004, and خ ر ب B001.** Blocking remembered naming is paired with active ruin; I infer a worship-space lives through voiced access, so silencing is architectural destruction.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 282 later protects public memory through writers and witnesses.

## 2:115 — وَلِلَّهِ ٱلْمَشْرِقُ وَٱلْمَغْرِبُ ۚ فَأَيْنَمَا تُوَلُّوا۟ فَثَمَّ وَجْهُ ٱللَّهِ
### Activated readings
1. **ش ر ق B001, غ ر ب B001, و ل ي B001, and و ج ه B001.** Opposite horizons remain within divine expanse; I infer directed turning is relational discipline, not spatial confinement of God.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 142-150 later make that distinction essential during the qiblah change.

## 2:116 — وَقَالُوا۟ ٱتَّخَذَ ٱللَّهُ وَلَدًۭا ... كُلٌّۭ لَّهُۥ قَٰنِتُونَ
### Activated readings
1. **و ل د B001 (generated child) and ق ن ت B001 (sustained obedience).** Kinship claim is replaced by total creaturely belonging; I infer universal dependence makes divine reproduction categorically unnecessary.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 233 and 286 later show created parenthood and burden as bounded, unlike divine sustaining rule.

## 2:117 — بَدِيعُ ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَٱلْأَرْضِ ... كُن فَيَكُونُ
### Activated readings
1. **ب د ع B001 (originating without precedent), ق ض ي B001, and ك و ن B001.** Decision and being collapse into a direct command-event; I infer creation needs no inherited template or lineage.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 258 later tests false sovereignty by moving from verbal claim to cosmic performance.

## 2:118 — وَقَالَ ٱلَّذِينَ لَا يَعْلَمُونَ ... تَشَٰبَهَتْ قُلُوبُهُمْ
### Activated readings
1. **ش ب ه B001, ق ل ب B001, and ي ق ن B001.** Similar demands disclose similar hearts across time; I infer sign-seeking can reproduce an inherited inward pattern, while clarified signs serve settled readers.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 260 later distinguishes a request for demonstration aimed at tranquility, not refusal.

## 2:119 — إِنَّآ أَرْسَلْنَٰكَ بِٱلْحَقِّ بَشِيرًۭا وَنَذِيرًۭا ...
### Activated readings
1. **ر س ل B001 (commissioned sending), ب ش ر B001, and ن ذ ر B001.** Promise and warning bound the messenger's task; I infer accountable transmission has limits, so recipients' outcome is not transferred to him.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 213 later places paired messengerial functions inside humanity's dispute-history.

## 2:120 — وَلَن تَرْضَىٰ عَنكَ ٱلْيَهُودُ ... وَلَا نَصِيرٍ
### Activated readings
1. **ر ض و B001, ت ب ع B001, م ل ل B001, and ه و ي B001.** Others' satisfaction requires following their communal path; I infer approval can become directional capture, so divine guidance must outrank audience desire.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 145-150 later stage conflicting directions and stabilize independent orientation.

## 2:121 — ٱلَّذِينَ ءَاتَيْنَٰهُمُ ٱلْكِتَٰبَ يَتْلُونَهُۥ حَقَّ تِلَاوَتِهِۦٓ ...
### Activated readings
1. **ت ل و B001 (following in sequence) and ح ق ق B001.** True recitation is due following, not sound alone; I infer the reader must enter the text's enacted sequence.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 285 later condenses this into hearing followed by obedience.

## 2:122 — يَٰبَنِىٓ إِسْرَٰٓءِيلَ ٱذْكُرُوا۟ نِعْمَتِىَ ...
### Activated readings
1. **ذ ك ر B003 and ف ض ل B003.** Repetition after dispute makes distinction a remembered gift, not permanent factional title.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 124 next denies covenantal reach to wrongdoers within ancestry.

## 2:123 — وَٱتَّقُوا۟ يَوْمًۭا لَّا تَجْزِى نَفْسٌ ...
### Activated readings
1. **ع د ل B003, ش ف ع B002, and ن ف ع B001.** Ransom, advocacy, benefit, and aid are sequentially closed; I infer inherited distinction cannot function as transferable credit.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 254 later closes sale, friendship, and intercession before the same day.

## 2:124 — ۞ وَإِذِ ٱبْتَلَىٰٓ إِبْرَٰهِۦمَ ... لَا يَنَالُ عَهْدِى ٱلظَّٰلِمِينَ
### Activated readings
1. **ب ل و B002, ت م م B001 (completion), ء م م B001 (leading), and ن ي ل B001 (attainment).** Completed tests precede leadership, while lineage request meets an ethical limit; I infer authority is performance-tested, not inherited automatically.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 247 later selects leadership against wealth-based entitlement.

## 2:125 — وَإِذْ جَعَلْنَا ٱلْبَيْتَ مَثَابَةًۭ لِّلنَّاسِ وَأَمْنًۭا ...
### Activated readings
1. **ث و ب B001 (return), ء م ن B001, ط و ف B001, and ع ك ف B001.** Returning, circling, staying, bowing, and prostrating organize secure space; I infer sanctuary is made by convergent movements and maintained purity.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayat 196-203 later elaborate these movements as timed communal discipline.

## 2:126 — وَإِذْ قَالَ إِبْرَٰهِۦمُ رَبِّ ٱجْعَلْ هَٰذَا بَلَدًا ءَامِنًۭا ...
### Activated readings
1. **ء م ن B001, ر ز ق B001, and م ت ع B001.** Security and fruit are requested for believers, yet temporal provision extends to rejecters; I infer worldly supply is wider than final approval.
### Retrospective surprises
Ayah 212 later states that provision is granted without the recipients' status calculus.

## 2:127 — وَإِذْ يَرْفَعُ إِبْرَٰهِۦمُ ٱلْقَوَاعِدَ مِنَ ٱلْبَيْتِ ...
### Activated readings
1. **ر ف ع B001, ق ع د B001 (foundation), and ق ب ل B004 (acceptance).** Physical elevation rests on bases while prayer seeks acceptance; I infer completed construction still depends on relational reception.

## 2:259 — أَوْ كَٱلَّذِى مَرَّ عَلَىٰ قَرْيَةٍۢ وَهِىَ خَاوِيَةٌ عَلَىٰ عُرُوشِهَا قَالَ أَنَّىٰ يُحْىِۦ هَٰذِهِ ٱللَّهُ بَعْدَ مَوْتِهَا ۖ فَأَمَاتَهُ ٱللَّهُ مِا۟ئَةَ عَامٍۢ ثُمَّ بَعَثَهُۥ ۖ قَالَ كَمْ لَبِثْتَ ۖ قَالَ لَبِثْتُ يَوْمًا أَوْ بَعْضَ يَوْمٍۢ ۖ قَالَ بَل لَّبِثْتَ مِا۟ئَةَ عَامٍۢ فَٱنظُرْ إِلَىٰ طَعَامِكَ وَشَرَابِكَ لَمْ يَتَسَنَّهْ ۖ وَٱنظُرْ إِلَىٰ حِمَارِكَ وَلِنَجْعَلَكَ ءَايَةًۭ لِّلنَّاسِ ۖ وَٱنظُرْ إِلَى ٱلْعِظَامِ كَيْفَ نُنشِزُهَا ثُمَّ نَكْسُوهَا لَحْمًۭا ۚ فَلَمَّا تَبَيَّنَ لَهُۥ قَالَ أَعْلَمُ أَنَّ ٱللَّهَ عَلَىٰ كُلِّ شَىْءٍۢ قَدِيرٌۭ

1. **ن ف ق B001 (a thing passes away or is cut off).** The root’s position within `ن ف ق → م و ل → ل ي ل → ن ه ر → س ر ر → ع ل ن → ء ج ر → ع ن د → ر ب ب → خ و ف → ح ز ن` makes it the entry into the ayah’s local sequence; I infer the following roots specify its effects or constraints, so the fixed ayah reads as an enacted relation rather than an isolated proposition.

No later ayah produced a distinct additional activation beyond the relations already retained.

## 2:275 — ٱلَّذِينَ يَأْكُلُونَ ٱلرِّبَوٰا۟ لَا يَقُومُونَ إِلَّا كَمَا يَقُومُ ٱلَّذِى يَتَخَبَّطُهُ ٱلشَّيْطَٰنُ مِنَ ٱلْمَسِّ ۚ ذَٰلِكَ بِأَنَّهُمْ قَالُوٓا۟ إِنَّمَا ٱلْبَيْعُ مِثْلُ ٱلرِّبَوٰا۟ ۗ وَأَحَلَّ ٱللَّهُ ٱلْبَيْعَ وَحَرَّمَ ٱلرِّبَوٰا۟ ۚ فَمَن جَآءَهُۥ مَوْعِظَةٌۭ مِّن رَّبِّهِۦ فَٱنتَهَىٰ فَلَهُۥ مَا سَلَفَ وَأَمْرُهُۥٓ إِلَى ٱللَّهِ ۖ وَمَنْ عَادَ فَأُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ أَصْحَٰبُ ٱلنَّارِ ۖ هُمْ فِيهَا خَٰلِدُونَ

## 2:276 — يَمْحَقُ ٱللَّهُ ٱلرِّبَوٰا۟ وَيُرْبِى ٱلصَّدَقَٰتِ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ لَا يُحِبُّ كُلَّ كَفَّارٍ أَثِيمٍ

## 2:282 — يَٰٓأَيُّهَا ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوٓا۟ إِذَا تَدَايَنتُم بِدَيْنٍ إِلَىٰٓ أَجَلٍۢ مُّسَمًّۭى فَٱكْتُبُوهُ ۚ وَلْيَكْتُب بَّيْنَكُمْ كَاتِبٌۢ بِٱلْعَدْلِ ۚ وَلَا يَأْبَ كَاتِبٌ أَن يَكْتُبَ كَمَا عَلَّمَهُ ٱللَّهُ ۚ فَلْيَكْتُبْ وَلْيُمْلِلِ ٱلَّذِى عَلَيْهِ ٱلْحَقُّ وَلْيَتَّقِ ٱللَّهَ رَبَّهُۥ وَلَا يَبْخَسْ مِنْهُ شَيْـًۭٔا ۚ فَإِن كَانَ ٱلَّذِى عَلَيْهِ ٱلْحَقُّ سَفِيهًا أَوْ ضَعِيفًا أَوْ لَا يَسْتَطِيعُ أَن يُمِلَّ هُوَ فَلْيُمْلِلْ وَلِيُّهُۥ بِٱلْعَدْلِ ۚ وَٱسْتَشْهِدُوا۟ شَهِيدَيْنِ مِن رِّجَالِكُمْ ۖ فَإِن لَّمْ يَكُونَا رَجُلَيْنِ فَرَجُلٌۭ وَٱمْرَأَتَانِ مِمَّن تَرْضَوْنَ مِنَ ٱلشُّهَدَآءِ أَن تَضِلَّ إِحْدَىٰهُمَا فَتُذَكِّرَ إِحْدَىٰهُمَا ٱلْأُخْرَىٰ ۚ وَلَا يَأْبَ ٱلشُّهَدَآءُ إِذَا مَا دُعُوا۟ ۚ وَلَا تَسْـَٔمُوٓا۟ أَن تَكْتُبُوهُ صَغِيرًا أَوْ كَبِيرًا إِلَىٰٓ أَجَلِهِۦ ۚ ذَٰلِكُمْ أَقْسَطُ عِندَ ٱللَّهِ وَأَقْوَمُ لِلشَّهَٰدَةِ وَأَدْنَىٰٓ أَلَّا تَرْتَابُوٓا۟ ۖ إِلَّآ أَن تَكُونَ تِجَٰرَةً حَاضِرَةًۭ تُدِيرُونَهَا بَيْنَكُمْ فَلَيْسَ عَلَيْكُمْ جُنَاحٌ أَلَّا تَكْتُبُوهَا ۗ وَأَشْهِدُوٓا۟ إِذَا تَبَايَعْتُمْ ۚ وَلَا يُضَآرَّ كَاتِبٌۭ وَلَا شَهِيدٌۭ ۚ وَإِن تَفْعَلُوا۟ فَإِنَّهُۥ فُسُوقٌۢ بِكُمْ ۗ وَٱتَّقُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ ۖ وَيُعَلِّمُكُمُ ٱللَّهُ ۗ وَٱللَّهُ بِكُلِّ شَىْءٍ عَلِيمٌۭ
### Activated readings
1. **ء م ن B001 (settled security and trust).** The root’s position within `ء م ن → د ي ن → د ي ن → ء ج ل → س م و → ك ت ب → ك ت ب → ب ي ن → ك ت ب → ع د ل → ء ب ي → ك ت ب → ك ت ب → ع ل م → ء ل ه → ك ت ب → م ل ل → ح ق ق → و ق ي → ء ل ه → ر ب ب → ب خ س → ش ي ء → ك و ن → ح ق ق → س ف ه → ض ع ف → ط و ع → م ل ل → م ل ل → و ل ي → ع د ل → ش ه د → ش ه د → ر ج ل → ك و ن → ر ج ل → ر ج ل → م ر ء → ر ض و → ش ه د → ض ل ل → ء ح د → ذ ك ر → ء ح د → ء خ ر → ء ب ي → ش ه د → د ع و → س ء م → ك ت ب → ص غ ر → ك ب ر → ء ج ل → ق س ط → ع ن د → ء ل ه → ق و م → ش ه د → د ن و → ر ي ب → ك و ن → ت ج ر → ح ض ر → د و ر → ب ي ن → ل ي س → ج ن ح → ك ت ب → ش ه د → ب ي ع → ض ر ر → ك ت ب → ش ه د → ف ع ل → ف س ق → و ق ي → ء ل ه → ع ل م → ء ل ه → ء ل ه → ك ل ل → ش ي ء → ع ل م` makes it the entry into the ayah’s local sequence; I infer the following roots specify its effects or constraints, so the fixed ayah reads as an enacted relation rather than an isolated proposition.
### Retrospective surprises
